### 📽 Evening sunlight pours through the dining hall’s western lattice in broad copper bands, glazing crowded tables, steaming bowls, and ward-etched pillars with the colour of banked fire; roasted roots, peppered meat, fresh bread, and sweet citrus mingle beneath the vaulted ceiling, while hundreds of overlapping conversations form a restless tide around one small pocket of reclaimed warmth.

# you asked

message time: 2026-08-02 19:10:36

List the names of all people encountered in this chapter and who they were

---

# chatgpt response

I’m compiling a chapter-wide cast list from the campaign files, separating named people from unnamed groups so nobody vanishes into the wallpaper.

## Named people directly encountered in Chapter 13

- **Jayson Goodwin / Shel’mekai’Vuran-Tah’aan**: The player character. A nine-tailed kitsune, former godlike figure, Axiomatic student, Pip’s adoptive father, and the person who invited Nyx to attend university.
- **Kestrel Thorne / Thrennael**: Jayson’s close friend and Axiomatic neighbour in Room 12. A calm warrior, practical guardian, and stabilizing presence.
- **Irix**: Jayson and Kestrel’s close friend. An Ashen Serathi assigned to Veiled House, with infernal features, wings, horns, and continuity-related abilities.
- **Elandria**: A goddess connected to Jayson's displacement. She returned four of his wives safely home, invited him into her divine realm, and introduced him to her family, household, nursery, and stables.
- **Pip**: A juvenile orchard mimic found in Elandria’s home. Jayson adopted her as his daughter. She calls him “Daa” or “Fok Daa.”
- **Nyx / Death**: The literal embodiment of Death across universes and one of the Four Riders. She entered the university disguised as a cheetah Felari under Candidate 024 and received an exceptional Triune Scholar charter. She was assigned Axiomatic Room 14. 
- **Aureliane**: Headmistress of Eryndor University. She personally intervened after Cael Vorr attempted to murder Pip and imposed severe institutional consequences.
- **Taren**: A young Wolfkin student who had been subjected to sustained intimidation by three other students. Jayson helped him, healed him, and encouraged his growing connection with Mira.
- **Mira**: A confident Wolfkin girl and Taren’s friend or developing romantic interest. She supported him after the confrontation and proved far more socially dangerous than he was prepared for. Jayson formally introduced the group to Taren and Mira later in the market. 
- **Cael Vorr**: An advanced Axiomatic adjudication student. He regarded Pip as vermin and threw a magically charged dining spike at her. Jayson caught it, restrained him, and Aureliane charged him with attempted murder and related offences. 

## Unnamed people directly encountered

- **Elandria’s husband**: A calm, scholarly man who lived with Elandria in her divine household and appeared comfortable teasing her. He helped explain the ancient horses’ presence. 
- **Elandria’s daughter**: Her older child, observant and willing to reveal embarrassing family details.
- **Elandria’s son**: Her younger child, energetic and equally willing to tease his mother.
- **The Master of Admissions**: The elderly university official who announced the admission results and Nyx’s Triune Scholar charter. 
- **Three House representatives**: Representatives of Axiomatic, Veiled, and Transcendent Houses present during the admissions ceremony.
- **Cael Vorr’s two companions**: Students who accompanied Cael during the confrontation and were later restrained for investigation.
- **Three students who intimidated Taren**: Unnamed aggressors confronted and removed under restraint.
- **An admissions clerk**: Injured by a desperate failed Felari candidate during the admissions ceremony.
- **University wardens and faculty staff**: Participated in restraining candidates, enforcing Aureliane’s orders, and managing admissions.
- **An unnamed student with another orchard mimic**: Inspired by Pip’s public acceptance, the student allowed their own previously hidden mimic to appear openly.

## Named people announced but not personally met

These names appeared on the admissions slate, but the chapter does not establish meaningful direct interaction with them:

- **Serin Vale**: Candidate 006, assigned to Axiomatic House.
- **Halden Ro**: Candidate 031, assigned to Axiomatic House.
- **Miraq Seth**: Candidate 078, assigned to Axiomatic House. 

## Mentioned but not encountered as people

- **War, Pestilence, and Famine**: The other Riders of the Apocalypse were discussed, but their riders were not personally met during the chapter.
- **Jayson’s wives**: Ayaka, Shion, Seika, Lysera, Varethyn, Naru, and Fei were discussed. Four were returned home by Elandria, but they did not directly appear in the chapter’s active scenes.
- **Solstice**: Nyx’s ancient white mare. She was directly encountered, but she is a horse and independent companion rather than a person.

**Document Title: **

Situation:

Chapter 13 ended after Jayson brought Pip back from Elandria’s divine realm and officially took responsibility for her as his daughter. Pip survived an attempted murder in the dining hall, where Cael Vorr threw a charged spike at her before Jayson caught it and Headmistress Aureliane intervened.

Nyx, the literal embodiment of Death, entered the university disguised as a cheetah Felari and was assigned Room 14 beside Jayson and Kestrel. With Nyx’s permission, Jayson revealed her true identity to Kestrel and Irix during a private evening in Irix’s Veiled dorm.

By the end of the night, Kestrel and Irix had accepted Nyx as part of the group, Pip was safely asleep in her drawer-bed, and Jayson, Kestrel, and Nyx had returned to their neighbouring rooms in Axiomatic House.
